Thus coordinate descent can be very fast using slice max. For each i:

Then set corr[n-1] = A0 + x - x*x where A0 = old_corr0 - old + old*old. Need old_corr0 before update.
But if multiple coordinate updates, h sum changes? Coordinate descent can change h[i] arbitrarily, breaking mass constraint! Previous coord_descent only box constrained, then project_feasible after. But projection changes all h and may increase max; maybe acceptable. However coordinate descent should enforce sum? We can include sum constraint in coordinate update? Since each h_i change changes sum. We could update h_i and maybe adjust j? Or allow mass drift and project periodically, but projection may undo improvements. Better to perform mass-preserving swaps: update one coordinate x and adjust another to keep sum, or project mass after each update? Previous coord_descent ignored sum during sweeps then projected; perhaps okay. But mass constraint important: ∫=1. If allow h_i arbitrary, objective could trivial? Maybe with sum not enforced, can set all h=0 to get zero overlap? Yes must enforce mass during optimization. Need incorporate sum.

Coordinate descent with sum exactly target: When changing h[i] from old to x, we need change total by Δ = x-old. We can compensate by adjusting another variable j (e.g., distributing over all others) or restrict update such that sum change zero (pair swap). Previous SLSQP enforced sum, binary greedy preserves n//2. Coordinate descent without sum then project can leave mass off and may exploit? It might still work but not exact; could produce infeasible optimum? Projection mass changes many variables potentially increasing C. But final project. However if mass not fixed during coordinate descent, it could find lower C at wrong mass; projection might not be feasible optimum. Better enforce mass.

Option 1: Coordinate descent on centered variables with sum fixed? For one coordinate, changing h_i requires changing others; difficult per coordinate. But we can formulate exact update of h_i given all other variables and mass? The other variables sum = target - old. If we set x, total mass constraint requires other sum = target - x, but they currently fixed sum target-old. Incoordinate descent with a single variable, we cannot change other variables. So either (a) perform update that keeps sum by combining x and a compensator y, or (b) allow temporary mass drift and project after each update, or (c) use direct constrained optimizer (SLSQP) with sum.

Gradient: For each active s with weight p_s, add p_s * grad_s. Need compute grad_s vector length n: for lag L, grad_s[j] = (1 - h[j-L]) if j-L valid? Wait g_s = c_s*dx where c_s = ∑_{p} h[p+L](1-h[p]) (np lag L). Derivative wrt h_j:
- as first factor: if j = p+L => p=j-L valid: derivative 1 - h[p] = 1 - h[j-L].
- as second factor: if j = p => p+L = j+L valid: derivative - h[p+L] = -h[j+L].
For L=0, both terms same variable: derivative (1-h_j) + (-h_j)=1-2h_j, consistent. So grad_s[j] = (1-h[j-L])_{valid} - h[j+L]_{valid}. This can be computed via shifts:
grad = np.zeros(n)
if L>=0:
  grad[:-L] += 1-h[L:]? Let's index: first factor condition j-L valid (0≤j-L<n) -> j ∈ [L,n-1], contributes 1 - h[j-L]. That's shift of 1-h by L: grad[L:] += 1 - h[:n-L]. Second factor j+L valid -> j∈[0,n-1-L], contributes -h[j+L]: grad[:n-L] -= h[L:].
If L<0, let q=-L: c_{-q} = ∑ h[p-q](1-h[p])? Derivative formula with L negative:
first j-L = j+q valid: j≤n-1-q, contributes 1-h[j+q]: grad[:n-q] += 1-h[q:]
second j+L = j-q valid: j≥q, contributes -h[j-q]: grad[q:] -= h[:n-q]
Or simply use formula with arrays:
For any L, grad = np.zeros(n)
If L >=0:
  g1 = 1 - h[:n-L]; add to grad[L:]
  g2 = -h[L:]; add to grad[:n-L]
If L <0:
  q=-L
  g1 = 1 - h[q:]; add to grad[:n-q]
  g2 = -h[:n-q]; add to grad[q:]
Check L=0: grad +=1-h - h=1-2h.
This is O(n) per significant lag.Weights p_s become small for large alpha; we can include only lags where p_s > eps (e.g. top active set). For alpha large, a few lags. We can compute p for all m (m small) and sort or threshold.

Let's examine exact coordinate descent with mass-preserving pair updates. Could be very powerful and can directly minimize max, not softplus. It can be run from fractional starts with many random pair swaps. Need design efficient and not too complex.

Pair update details:
Given h length n, corr length m=2n-1 (coeff). Sum target n/2. For chosen i,j, old x=h[i], y=h[j]. Let δ ∈ [δmin,δmax] where δmin=max(-x, y-1), δmax=min(1-x, 1-y). New x'=x+δ, y'=y-δ.

Let's write gradient via active loops:
```
def obj_grad(h, n, alpha):
    g = np.correlate(h, 1-h, 'full') * (2.0/n)
    gm = g.max()
    e = np.exp(alpha*(g-gm))
    S = e.sum()
    f = gm + np.log(S)/alpha
    p = e/S
    active = np.where(p > max(1e-14, 0.5/m?))[0]
    grad = np.zeros(n)
    for idx in active:
       w = p[idx]
       if w < 1e-14: continue
       L = idx - (n-1)
       if L >=0:
          if L==0:
             grad += w*(1-2*h)
          else:
             grad[L:] += w*(1-h[:n-L])
             grad[:n-L] -= w*h[L:]
       else:
          q=-L
          grad[:n-q] += w*(1-h[q:])
          grad[q:] -= w*h[:n-q]
    return f, grad
```
Check gradient of F wrt h? F = logsumexp(α g)/α, p normalized. g coeff dx. For L=0, grad of g0: g0 = ∑ h(1-h)dx, derivative dx*(1-2h). In our formula with L=0: (1-2h)*dx? I omitted dx. Need multiply by dx. In g we multiplied dx. For L≠0, derivative of g_L = dx * sum products; gradient entries dx *(...). Our slice additions should include dx. So either p weights and add w * dx *... Or precompute g without dx and objective multiplies dx, gradient dx factor. Easier: compute corr no dx, f = (logsumexp(α (corr*dx)))/α, gradient = dx * ∑ p ∇corr. Include dx in loops. Or scale grad by dx after. We'll multiply grad by dx at end.

Algorithm PG:
```
def optimize_pg(h,n,alpha,deadline,max_iter,step_init=...):
  h = project_feasible(h,n)
  best=...
  step = 0.1
  for it:
    f, grad = obj_grad(h,n,alpha)
    # project gradient onto tangent sum=0? Projected gradient onto box+hyperplane can be done by first do gradient step then project.
    for ls in 10:
       cand = project_feasible(h - step*grad, n)
       fc, _ = obj_only(cand)
       if fc < f - 1e-12 or it? accept
       step *=0.5
    if accepted:
       h=cand; if fc<best: best...
       step *=1.05
    else: break
```

Alternatively use sequential convex programming: At current h, approximate objective by active max lags and solve a linearized problem? Since g_s(h) is bilinear, linearize around current h: g_s(h+e) ≈ g_s + ∇g_s·e. Minimize t subject to t ≥ g_s + ∇g_s·e for active s, bounds sum. This is a convex QP (linear constraints). Solve with scipy linprog? Variables n + t, many constraints and bounds + equality. Use scipy.optimize.linprog? It's linear. But linear constraints with equality sum, bounds. However we can solve QP? The objective is t linear; min t with linear inequalities -> LP! But the linearized approximation uses equality? We can solve LP to find next h. But g_s also has bilinear term; linearization local. This is like prox-linear. We can solve LP using scipy.optimize.linprog (HiGHS) which is fast for n=400, constraints 800? Variables n+1=401, constraints (2n-1) ≤ t -> A h - t ≤ -g_s? plus equality. A matrix m x n dense maybe 799*400=320k, okay. linprog high res maybe slow but okay. But linearized constraints may not preserve true objective (can increase). We can line-search from current to LP solution to reduce actual F.

This could be powerful: active-set linearized minimax. For each iteration:
1. Compute g and pick active lags (top few, maybe all with g > max - ε).
2. Solve LP:
   minimize t
   subject to for each active s: ∇g_s(h)·e ≤ t - g_s (since g + grad·e ≤ t)
   h_new = h + e, 0≤h_new≤1, sum=e sum 0.
   Use variables (h,t) directly: minimize t, constraints ∇g_s·h - t ≤ g_s - grad_at? Wait linearization around h0: g_s(h) ≈ g0_s + ∇s·(h-h0) = (∇s)·h + (g0_s - ∇s·h0). Constraint ∇s·h - t ≤ g0_s - ∇s·h0.
   Sum h=S, bounds.

Implement project_feasible efficiently:
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          mask=h<1.0
          s=(1-h)[mask].sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h[mask] += diff*(1-h[mask])/s
       else:
          mask=h>0.0
          s=h[mask].sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h[mask] += diff*h[mask]/s
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact adjust
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]... add small to first feasible
    elif diff<0:
       idx=np.argsort(h) ... subtract
    return h
```
Could be O(n log n) final; okay. But in loops many calls, use simpler O(n): maintain? Projected gradient line search calls many times; need faster. Implement bisection projection? But project_feasible above O(n*50) each. For many calls, heavy. We need efficient projection.

Projection onto [0,1], sum=target can be done with bisection on λ:
find λ such that sum(clip(x-λ,0,1))=target. Since target between0,n, λ unique. Use binary 60 iterations O(n). For project_feasible for w:
```
def project_feasible(x,n):
  lo=min(x)-1; hi=max(x)
  for 60: mid; s=sum(clip(x-mid,0,1)); if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
  return clip(x-mid,0,1)
  final adjust one element
```
This is O(60n) but similar. The iterative redistribution above O(n*few) may be faster if x already near feasible. For SLSQP end, okay. For line search, h+λ(p-h) already exactly feasible if p and h are (LP sum exactly), bounds maybe; no need project. For PG, yes. We might not need project often.

Let's implement softplus objective with active gradient:
Globals: `GOBJ_N = 0`, `GOBJ_ALPHA = 1.0`, `GOBJ_MAXL=32`.
```
def soft_objective(x):
    n=GOBJ_N
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x, 1-x, 'full')
    gmax=c.max()
    val=gmax*dx
    al=GOBJ_ALPHA
    if al <= 0: return val
    # For alpha large, shift by max coeff (not gmax*dx?) to avoid huge: cmax = c.max()
    z = al*(c - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    S = np.exp(z).sum()
    return val + np.log(S)*dx/al
```
But gradient weights based on p = exp(z)/S. For alpha huge, z underflow; okay.
```
def soft_gradient(x):
   n=GOBJ_N; dx=2.0/n; al=GOBJ_ALPHA
   c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
   z=al*(c-c.max())
   z=np.clip(z,-50,0) # max zero
   e=np.exp(z); S=e.sum()
   p=e/S
   active_idx=np.where(p > 1e-8)[0]
   if active_idx.size ==0: active_idx=[c.argmax()]
   if active_idx.size > GOBJ_MAXL:
       # top MAXL by p
       order=np.argsort(p)[::-1][:GOBJ_MAXL]
       active_idx=order
   # renormalize p active
   pa=p[active_idx].sum()
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   zlag=n-1
   for idx in active_idx:
      w=p[idx]/pa*dx
      L=idx-zlag
      if L==0:
         grad += w*(1-2*x)
      elif L>0:
         if L<n:
            grad[L:] += w*(1-x[:n-L])
            grad[:n-L] -= w*x[L:]
      else:
         q=-L
         grad[:n-q] += w*(1-x[q:])
         grad[q:] -= w*x[:n-q]
   return grad
```
Wait objective f = logsumexp(α c dx)/α? I used c not multiplied dx in exponent but final val uses dx. If g_s = c_s dx, logsumexp(α g)/α = (1/α) log∑exp(α dx c). If we use shift cmax and final val cmax*dx + dx/α log S. Derivative = ∑ p_s ∇(c_s dx) = dx ∑p_s ∇c_s. Good.
Using alpha in terms of c not dx? If α small? In previous code alpha on g (with dx), equivalently α*dx on c. Previous alphas up to 2e8 relative to g ~0.38. Our alpha on c ~80? Equivalent: g=dx*c, n=400 dx0.005, α_g = α_c? If we use exponent α(c-cmax), equivalent α_g*g? α_c = α_g*dx? For α_g=50, α_c=0.25 too small spread. Need choose α_c appropriately. Let's define `_ALPHA` as alpha for g (C units). Then exponent = _ALPHA*(c*dx - cmax*dx)=_ALPHA*dx*(c-cmax). So set lam = _ALPHA * dx. In code, compute `lam = float(GOBJ_ALPHA) * (2.0/n)`. z=lam*(c-cmax). Final f = cmax*dx + (lam? log S)/_ALPHA? Since f = (1/alpha) log∑exp(α dx(c-cmax)) + cmax dx = cmax dx + log S / alpha. Yes. Derivative = ∑ p dx ∇c. So use lam = alpha*dx for z, weights normalized; gradient multiply dx. That matches previous alpha scale. Good. We'll use `lam = GOBJ_ALPHA * dx`.
If alpha large, lam large; clip z to [-700,0] safe.

LP solve:
```
def affine_step(h,n,K=32,trust=0.1):
  c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=float(c.max())
  active = top lags
  A=[]; b=[]
  for idx in active:
    L=idx-(n-1); gr=lag_gradient(h,n,L); A.append(gr); b.append(c[idx] - float(gr.dot(h)))
  A=np.array(A) + column -1
  c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[n]=1
  A_eq=np.ones(n+1); A_eq[n]=0
  b_eq=n/2
  lb=np.maximum(0,h-trust); ub=np.minimum(1,h+trust); t bounds (0,None)
  res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=list(zip(lb,ub))+[(0,None)])
  if not success: return h,cmax
  ph=res.x[:-1]; t_lp=res.x[-1]
  # possibly ph not exactly feasible; clip/project? It should.
  # line search
  best=h; bestd=cmax
  for lam in np.array([1,0.8,0.6,0.4,0.25,0.125,0.0625]):
     cand=h+lam*(ph-h)
     # ensure feasible: if sum error >1e-8, project? But convex combination feasible; bounds if ph within trust.
     cc=float(np.max(np.correlate(cand,1-cand,'full')))
     if cc < bestd - 1e-12:
        best=cand; bestd=cc
        break
  else:
     # if small positive try 0? no
  return best,bestd
```

cur=h.copy(); curc=compute_c5
for trust in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
  for it in range(maxit):
    if time>deadline break
    cand,candc=affine_step(cur,n,K=32,trust=trust)
    if candc < curc - 1e-13:
       cur=cand; curc=candc
    else: break
  if time>deadline break
return cur,curc
```

Details:
```
def low_block_obj(x):
    p,q=x
    A=G_A;B=G_B;C=G_C;n=G_NL
    if C <=0: return 1e6
    r=(n/2 - 2*A*p -2*B*q)/C
    if p<-1e-8 or p>1+1e-8 or q... or r...: return 1e6
    # build h efficiently without allocating each time? use fixed template? Could allocate.
    h=np.empty(n)
    h[:A]=p; h[A:A+B]=q; h[A+B:A+B+C]=r;
    off=A+B+C
    h[off:off+B]=q; h[off+B:]=p
    return compute_c5(h,n)
```
But for high n_low maybe n=128, okay. To reduce allocations, G_TEMPLATE? Can't easily fill different slices without allocate. Fine.

Enumeration:
```
def low_block_search(n, deadline):
  besth=None;bestc=inf
  maxA=n//4; maxB=(n//2)
  for A in range(0,maxA+1):
    rem=n-2*A
    if rem<1: continue
    # C=rem-2*B; ensure C>=1 and parity? We can step B such that C>=1
    maxB=rem//2
    for B in range(0,maxB+1):
       C=rem-2*B
       if C<1: continue
       G_A=A;G_B=B;G_C=C;G_NL=n
       # starts: grid 4x4? To save, use initial [1,0], [0.5,0.5], [0.25,0.25]
       # choose from a small grid of p,q to seed
       ps=(0.0,0.5,1.0)
       qs=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)
       beststart=None;bestval=inf
       for p in ps:
         for q in qs:
           val=low_block_obj([p,q])
           if val<bestval: ...
       if bestval>10: continue
       # Use Nelder-Mead up to maybe 30 evaluations
       res=opt.minimize(low_block_obj, beststart, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':30, 'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-6})
       xbest=res.x; val=low_block_obj(xbest)
       if val<bestc: construct h, bestc=val
  return besth,bestc
```
Number of triples for n=128: A 0..32, B 0..(128-2A)/2, C≥1. Sum ~2184? For each grid 15 +30=45 eval -> 98k eval. correlate length128 (255) ~ maybe 0.03ms? 98k*0.05ms=5s? `active_indices(c,K)`:
```
def active_indices(c,K):
  m=len(c); idx=np.argmax(c)
  cmax=c[idx]
  tol=max(1e-12, 1e-7*cmax)
  cand=np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - tol)
  if cand.size > K:
      order=np.argsort(c[cand])[::-1][:K]
      cand=cand[order]
  else:
      if idx not in cand: cand=np.r_[cand,idx]
  return cand
```

SLSQP once: We can't define nested; define `slsqp_once(h,n,alpha,maxiter)`:
```
def slsqp_once(h,n,alpha,maxiter,ftol):
    global GOBJ_N, GOBJ_ALPHA, G_N
    GOBJ_N=n; GOBJ_ALPHA=float(alpha); G_N=n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint}]
    try:
      res=opt.minimize(soft_objective, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=constraints, jac=soft_gradient, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':ftol})
    except Exception:
      return h
    x=res.x
    # if not finite, return h
    return project_feasible(np.clip(x,0,1),n)
```
But `minimize` may call sum_constraint with x of shape (n,), yes.

Detailed functions:

```
def compute_ccoeff(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)
def c5_of_h(h,n):
    return float(np.max(compute_ccoeff(h)) * (2.0/n))
```
But compute_ccoeff returns full. In c5, `return float(np.max(np.correlate(...))* (2/n))`.

Project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          s=room.sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h=h + (diff/s)*room
       else:
          s=h.sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h=h + (diff/s)*h
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
        idx=np.argmax(1-h)
        if 1-h[idx] >= diff:
            h[idx]+=diff
        else:
            # distribute
            h=project_feasible(h,n)?? recursion risk. fallback
    elif diff<0:
        idx=np.argmax(h)
        if -h[idx] > diff: h[idx]+=diff (diff negative subtract)
    return h
```
The iterative update may slightly over/under due to floating. Final adjust with idx. If diff small <= room. For diff negative, if abs(diff) <= h[idx], h[idx] += diff. If not, do one more redistribution? Use loop 5 already. If not, clip and accept. Error tiny.
But if h all 1 and diff>0 impossible due target n/2≤n; if target n/2, not all 1. Fine.

Soft value/gradient:
```
def soft_value_grad(x):
    n=G_N; dx=2.0/n; alpha=G_ALPHA
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full').astype(float)
    cmax=float(c.max())
    val=cmax*dx
    if alpha <= 0:
       grad=(1.0-2.0*x if cmax at 0? no hard max not; use active only top lags? We won't call alpha 0)
    lam=alpha*dx
    z=lam*(c-cmax)
    z=np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e=np.exp(z)
    S=e.sum()
    if S <=0: S=1; e[c.argmax()]=1
    p=e/S
    val += np.log(S)/alpha
    active=np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-12)
    if active.size > G_MAXL:
        # top by p
        active=active[np.argsort(p[active])[::-1][:G_MAXL]]
    if active.size ==0:
        active=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
    wa=p[active].sum()
    if wa<=0: wa=1.0
    grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    zlag=n-1
    for idx in active:
       w=float(p[idx]/wa)*dx
       L=int(idx)-zlag
       if L==0:
          grad += w*(1.0-2.0*x)
       elif L>0:
          if L<n:
             grad[L:] += w*(1.0 - x[:n-L])
             grad[:n-L] -= w*x[L:]
       else:
          q=-L
          grad[:n-q] += w*(1.0 - x[q:])
          grad[q:] -= w*x[:n-q]
    return float(val), grad
```
Set `G_ALPHA` positive. If alpha huge, lam*diff may overflow? z clipped after multiplication; use with numpy may overflow warning if lam=1e8 and diff=1000 ->1e11; not if n small? lam = alpha*dx, for alpha=1e8,n=64 dx=0.031 ->3e6, times cmax diff maybe <=n=64 ->2e8; exp clip after, but z produced large negative finite; okay no overflow positive since ≤0. Could be -inf if underflow multiplication? no.
To avoid warning, `z = np.minimum(0, lam*(c-cmax)); z = np.maximum(z, -700)`.

G_MAXL set e.g. 256. Active all if p small threshold; for low alpha p~1/m; if m=799, threshold1e-12 includes all 799; G_MAXL 256 drops. If alpha 100, lam=0.005? Wait n=400 dx=0.005, alpha=100 -> lam=0.5. c differences around maybe 1, active within 2 -> e^{-0.5}, e^{-1}; many. G_MAXL 256 enough? m799. There could be ~100 active. Good. For n=64,m127 all <256, no drop. Set G_MAXL=512 to be safe.

SLSQP function with global G_N set to n. For constraint, no kwargs.
```
def run_slsqp(h,n,alpha,maxiter,deadline):
    global G_N,G_ALPHA,G_MAXL
    G_N=n; G_ALPHA=float(alpha); G_MAXL=512
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    constraints=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_constraint}]
    try:
       res=opt.minimize(soft_objective,h,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=constraints,jac=soft_gradient, options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'disp':0})
       if res.success or hasattr(res,'x'): ...
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       return h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return h
    return project_feasible(np.clip(x,0,1),n)
```
Need `soft_gradient` top-level, not `soft_gradient` uses G_N/alpha. Good.

But minimize with `jac=soft_gradient` will call with x, returns gradient. In SLSQP, constraints Jacobian finite diff for equality; `sum_constraint` simple.

def pen_objective(x):
   v,_=soft_value_grad(x); res=x.sum()-G_N/2; return v+0.5*G_RHO*res*res
def pen_gradient(x):
   _,g=soft_value_grad(x); res=x.sum()-G_N/2; return g+2*G_RHO*res

PG function using `soft_value_grad` one call:
```
def pg_step(h,n,step):
   f,g=soft_value_grad(h)
   return project_feasible(h - step*g,n), f
def pg_optimize(h,n,alpha,deadline,max_iter,step):
    global G_ALPHA
    G_ALPHA=float(alpha)
    cur=h.copy(); curc=c5_of_h(cur,n); best=cur; bestc=curc
    fails=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       f,g=soft_value_grad(cur)
       cand=project_feasible(cur - step*g,n)
       cc=c5_of_h(cand,n)
       accepted=False
       while step>1e-6 and cc > curc - 1e-13:
           step*=0.5
           cand=project_feasible(cur - step*g,n)
           cc=c5_of_h(cand,n)
       if cc < curc - 1e-13:
           cur=cand; curc=cc; if curc<bestc: best=cur; bestc=curc
           step=min(step*1.1, 1.0); fails=0
       else:
           fails+=1
           if fails>5: break
    return best,bestc

GB_A=0;GB_B=0;GB_C=0;GB_N=0
def build_block(A,B,C,n,p,q,r):
    h=np.empty(n)
    off=0
    h[:A]=p; off=A
    h[off:off+B]=q; off+=B
    h[off:off+C]=r; off+=C
    h[off:off+B]=q; off+=B
    h[off:]=p
    return h
def block_obj(x):
  p,q=x; A=GB_A;B=GB_B;C=GB_C;n=GB_N
  if C<=0: return 10
  r=(n/2 - 2*A*p -2*B*q)/C
  if p<-1e-8 or p>1+1e-8 or q... or r...: return 10
  h=build_block(...)
  return c5_of_h(h,n)
```

def low_block_search(n,deadline):
    besth=None;bestc=1e100
    GB_N=n
    maxA=n//4; maxB=n//2
    # include deterministic equal-ish: for step around n/5
    candidates=[]
    # deterministic: A from 0.1n to0.3n, B from0.05n to0.25n maybe
    for A in range(max(0,int(0.05*n)), min(maxA+1,int(0.30*n)+1)):
       for B in range(max(0,int(0.02*n)), min(maxB+1,int(0.30*n)+1)):
          C=n-2*A-2*B
          if C>=1: candidates.append((A,B,C))
    # if many, sample
    if len(candidates)>800:
       rng=np.random.RandomState(0)
       idx=rng.choice(len(candidates),800,replace=False)
       candidates=[candidates[i] for i in idx]
    for A,B,C in candidates:
       if time.time()>deadline-5: break
       GB_A=A;GB_B=B;GB_C=C
       # small grid starts
       starts=[]
       for p in (0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          for q in (0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
              val=block_obj([p,q])
              starts.append((val,[p,q]))
       starts.sort(key=lambda z:z[0]) # no lambda allowed!
```

def optimize_candidate(hold, n, deadline):
    global G_N
    G_N=int(n)

          h=x;c=cx
    # LP polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 8:

    hs=...
    return h,c

def lbfgsb_soft(h,n,alpha,maxiter):
  global G_N,G_ALPHA,G_RHO
  G_N=n;G_ALPHA=float(alpha);G_RHO=1000.0
  bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
  try:
    res=minimize(pen_objective,h,method='L-BFGS-B',jac=pen_gradient,bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':int(maxiter),'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxfun':int(maxiter*10)})
    x=res.x
  except Exception: return h
  if not finite return h
  return np.clip(x,0,1)

`affine_step` details with linprog. Need if `linprog` not available or solve fails. Use `try`.
```
def affine_step(h,n,K=32,trust=0.05):
   c=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full'); cmax=float(c.max())
   idxs=active_indices(c,K)
   if idxs.size==0: return h,cmax
   rows=[]; rhs=[]
   for idx in idxs:
       L=int(idx)-(n-1)
       gr=lag_gradient(h,n,L)
       rows.append(gr)
       rhs.append(float(gr.dot(h)) - float(c[idx]))
   A=np.vstack(rows).astype(float)
   b=np.array(rhs,float)
   A_ub=np.empty((A.shape[0],n+1))
   A_ub[:,:n]=A
   A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
   b_ub=b # since ∇h - t ≤ ∇h0 - c => A h + (-1)t ≤ b
   c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[n]=1.0
   A_eq=np.ones(n+1); A_eq[n]=0.0
   b_eq=n/2.0
   lb=np.maximum(0.0,h-trust); ub=np.minimum(1.0,h+trust)
   bounds=list(zip(lb,ub)) + [(0.0,None)]
   res=linprog(c_obj,A_ub=A_ub,b_ub=b_ub,A_eq=A_eq,b_eq=b_eq,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':5?})
```
`linprog` options may not accept time_limit? HiGHS has `time_limit` maybe. We can omit to avoid unknown. Could set `{'presolve': True}`. If linprog takes long, no. K=32,n=400; should be fast. Use `method='highs'` if available; older default. We can try `linprog(..., options={'presolve': False})`? Some options unknown cause warning but works? Safer no options.
If success: `ph=res.x[:-1]; t_lp=res.x[-1]`.

       cand=np.clip(cand,0,1)

`active_indices` function:
```
def active_indices(c,K):
    m=c.size
    cmax=float(c.max())
    tol=max(1e-12, 0.02) # coeff units? 0.02 tiny; top K
    cand=np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - tol)
    if cand.size > K:
       cand=cand[np.argsort(c[cand])[::-1][:K]]
    if int(np.argmax(c)) not in cand.tolist(): cand=np.r_[cand,np.argmax(c)]
    return cand
```
0.02 coeff too small; topK enough. Use `tol=max(1e-9, 1e-6*cmax)` basically top after K. If all unique, cand may be 1. Upper K? We want include near ties. Use `tol=max(1e-8, 0.01*cmax)`. For cmax~80, tol0.8, includes lags within 0.8 coeff (C diff 0.001 for n=400). Good. K=32. Good.

`affine_loop`:
```
def affine_loop(h,n,deadline):
   cur=h.copy(); curc=c5_of_h(cur,n)
   K=24 if n<150 else 36
   trusts=[0.10,0.06,0.03,0.02,0.01]
   for tr in trusts:
      if time.time()>deadline-5: break
      it=0
      while time.time()<deadline-5 and it<25:
         cand,candc=affine_step(cur,n,K,tr)
         it+=1
         if candc < curc - 1e-13:
            cur=cand; curc=candc
         else:
            break
   return cur,curc
```

```
def c5_of_h(h,n):
    if h is None or len(h)!=n: return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return 1e100
    c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,'full')
    return float(np.max(c)*(2.0/n))
```

```
def low_block_search(n, deadline):
    global GB_N
    GB_N=n
    besth=None; bestc=1e100
    maxA=n//4; maxB=n//2
    pairs=[]
    # deterministic near equal
    A0=n//5; B0=n//10
    for dA in range(-3,4):
      for dB in range(-3,4):
         A=A0+dA*(n//20); B=B0+dB*(n//20)
         if 0<=A<=maxA and 0<=B<=maxB:
            C=n-2*A-2*B
            if C>=1: pairs.append((A,B,C))
    rng=np.random.RandomState(7)
    for _ in range(1200):
       A=int(rng.randint(0,maxA+1)); B=int(rng.randint(0,maxB+1)); C=...
       if C>=1: pairs.append((A,B,C))
    # deduplicate? We'll process all; duplicates waste. Use set?
```

```
    seen=set(); utmp=[]

       if p not in seen:

    pairs=utmp
```

       start_pairs=[]; vals=[]
       for pp in (0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
          for qq in (0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0):
             start_pairs.append((pp,qq))
             vals.append(block_obj((pp,qq)))
       vals=np.array(vals); order=np.argsort(vals)
       for _o in range(min(2, len(order))):
          start=start_pairs[int(order[_o])]
          val0=float(vals[int(order[_o])])
          if val0 > 2.0: break  # bad, no local optimum? maybe
          try:
             res=minimize(block_obj, start, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-6,'maxfunc':80})
             xbest=res.x
             val=block_obj(xbest)
          except Exception:
             val=val0; xbest=start
          if val < bestc:
             p,q=xbest; r=(n/2 - 2*A*p -2*B*q)/C
             if p>-1e-7 and p<1+1e-7 and q...:
                h=build_block(A,B,C,n,float(np.clip(p,0,1)),float(np.clip(q,0,1)),float(np.clip(r,0,1)))
                besth=project_feasible(h,n); bestc=c5_of_h(besth,n)
    return besth,bestc
```

def make_smooth(n, kind):
    idx=np.arange(n,float); c=n/2.0; u=np.abs(idx-(c-0.5))/c
    if kind==0: v=1.0-u
    elif kind==1: v=1.0-0.5*u*u
    elif kind==2: v=np.exp(-1.5*u*u)
    else: v=1.0 - 0.75*u**1.4
    v=np.clip(v,0.0,1.0)
    return v
```

def optimize_candidate(hold,n,deadline):
    global G_N,G_ALPHA
    G_N=int(n); n=G_N
    h=project_feasible(hold,n)
    c=c5_of_h(h,n)
    hs=project_feasible(symmetrize(h,n),n); cs=c5_of_h(hs,n)
    if cs<c: h=hs;c=cs
    # LBFGS cycles
    if n > 300: alphas=(100.0,500.0,3000.0,20000.0); maxiters=(30,40,50,70)
    elif n > 160: alphas=(100.0,500.0,3000.0,20000.0,200000.0); maxiters=(50,70,90,120,140)
    else: alphas=(100.0,500.0,3000.0,20000.0,200000.0,2000000.0); maxiters=(80,100,120,150,180,180)
    for i,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
       if time.time() > deadline - 10: break
       x=lbfgsb_soft(h,n,alpha,int(maxiters[i]))
       x=project_feasible(x,n)
       cx=c5_of_h(x,n)
       xs=project_feasible(symmetrize(x,n),n); cx2=c5_of_h(xs,n)
       if cx2<cx: x=xs;cx=cx2
       if cx < c - 1e-12:
          h=x;c=cx
    # SLSQP if n small and time
    if n <= 170 and time.time() < deadline - 25:
       x=slsqp_soft(h,n,50000.0,120)
       x=project_feasible(x,n)
       cx=c5_of_h(x,n)
       xs=...
       if cx<c: h=x;c=cx
    # LP polish
    if time.time() < deadline - 15:
       h,c=affine_loop(h,n,deadline)
    hs=...
    return h,c
```

def low_search(n, deadline):
    global GB_N
    GB_N=n
    besth=None; bestc=1e100
    maxA=n//4; maxB=n//2
    seen=set(); triples=[]
    A0=n//5; B0=n//5
    for dA in range(-3,4):
      for dB in range(-3,4):
        A=A0+dA*max(1,n//20); B=B0+dB*max(1,n//20)
        C=n-2*A-2*B
        if 0<=A<=maxA and 0<=B<=maxB and C>=1:
           t=(A,B,C)
           if t not in seen: seen.add(t); triples.append(t)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(3)
    for _ in range(120):
       A=int(rng.randint(0,maxA+1)); B=int(rng.randint(0,maxB+1)); C=...
       if C>=1: ...
    if len(triples)>160:
       idx=np.random.RandomState(11).choice(len(triples),160,replace=False)
       triples=[triples[int(i)] for i in idx]
    for t in triples:
       if time.time()>deadline-8: break
       GB_A,GB_B,GB_C=t
       ps=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0); qs=(0.0,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0)
       vals=[]; starts=[]
       for p in ps:
         for q in qs:
           val=block_obj((p,q)); vals.append(val); starts.append((p,q))
       vals=np.array(vals); oi=int(np.argmin(vals))
       if vals[oi]>=2.0: continue
       try: res=minimize(block_obj,starts[oi],method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxfunc':35,'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-6})
       except: res=None
       if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           x=res.x; val=block_obj(x)
       else: x=starts[oi]; val=vals[oi]
       if val<bestc:
          p,q=float(x[0]),float(x[1]); r=(n/2-2*GB_A*p-2*GB_B*q)/GB_C
          if 0<=p<=1 and ...:
             h=build_block(GB_A,GB_B,GB_C,n,float(np.clip(p,0,1)),...)
             h=proj(h,n); c=c5(h,n)
             if c<bestc: bestc=c; besth=h
    return besth,bestc

tests=[]

for target in (256,192,160,128,96):

`optimize` compact:
```
def optimize(hold,n,dead):
  G_N=int(n); n=G_N
  h=proj(hold,n); c=c5(h,n)
  hs=proj(sym(h,n),n); cs=c5(hs,n)
  if cs<c: h=hs; c=cs
  if n>300: alphas=(100,500,3000,20000); mits=(30,40,50,60)
  elif n>160: alphas=(100,500,3000,20000,200000); mits=(50,60,80,100,120)
  else: alphas=(100,500,3000,20000,100000); mits=(70,80,100,120,120)
  for i in range(len(alphas)):
     if time.time()>dead-12: break
     x=lbfgs(h,n,alphas[i],mits[i])
     x=proj(x,n); cx=c5(x,n)
     xs=proj(sym(x,n),n); csx=c5(xs,n)
     if csx<cx: x=xs; cx=csx
     if cx<c-1e-12: h=x; c=cx
  if time.time()<dead-18:
     h,c=lp_loop(h,n,dead)
  hs=proj(sym(h,n),n); cs=c5(hs,n)
  if cs<c: h=hs;c=cs
  return h,c
```

def proj(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(40):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          s=(1-h).sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h+=diff*(1-h)/s
       else:
          s=h.sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h+=diff*h/s
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
    elif diff<0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h)); h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

G_N = 0
G_ALPHA = 1.0
G_MAXL = 512
G_RHO = 500.0

GB_A = 0
GB_B = 0
GB_C = 0
GB_N = 0

BEST_H = None
BEST_C = 1e100
BEST_N = 0


def c5(h, n):
    if h is None or len(h) != n:
        return 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return 1e100
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))


def proj(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = n / 2.0
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            s = (1.0 - h).sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + diff * (1.0 - h) / s
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + diff * h / s
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
            h[idx] -= sub
    return h


def sym(h):
    return 0.5 * (h + h[::-1])


def consider(h, n):
    global BEST_H, BEST_C, BEST_N
    if h is None or n <= 0:
        return
    n = int(n)
    h = proj(np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    cur = c5(h, n)
    hs = proj(sym(h), n)
    cs = c5(hs, n)
    if cs < cur:
        h = hs
        cur = cs
    if cur < BEST_C - 1e-12:
        BEST_H = h.copy()
        BEST_C = cur
        BEST_N = n


def interp_to(src, n0, n1):
    if src is None or n0 <= 0:
        return np.full(n1, 0.5)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n0))
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n1))
    return np.interp(x1, x0, np.asarray(src, dtype=float))


def soft_valgrad(x):
    n = int(G_N)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    alpha = float(G_ALPHA)
    ctrl = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full').astype(float)
    cmax = float(np.max(ctrl))
    val = cmax * dx
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        return val, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    lam = alpha * dx
    z = lam * (ctrl - cmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    S = float(e.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        e[int(np.argmax(ctrl))] = 1.0
        S = 1.0
    p = e / S
    val += np.log(S) / alpha
    active = np.flatnonzero(p > 1e-12)
    if active.size > G_MAXL:
        active = active[np.argsort(p[active])[::-1][:G_MAXL]]
    if active.size == 0:
        active = np.array([int(np.argmax(ctrl))])
    wa = float(p[active].sum())
    if wa <= 0.0:
        wa = 1.0
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    z0 = n - 1
    for idx in active:
        w = float(p[idx] / wa) * dx
        L = int(idx) - z0
        if L == 0:
            g += w * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        elif L > 0:
            g[L:] += w * (1.0 - x[:n - L])
            g[:n - L] -= w * x[L:]
        else:
            q = -L
            g[:n - q] += w * (1.0 - x[q:])
            g[q:] -= w * x[:n - q]
    return float(val), g


def pen_obj(x):
    v, _ = soft_valgrad(x)
    r = float(x.sum() - G_N / 2.0)
    return float(v + 0.5 * G_RHO * r * r)


def pen_grad(x):
    _, g = soft_valgrad(x)
    r = float(x.sum() - G_N / 2.0)
    return g + 2.0 * G_RHO * r


def smooth_obj(x):
    return soft_valgrad(x)[0]


def smooth_grad(x):
    return soft_valgrad(x)[1]


def lbfgs_step(h, n, alpha, iters):
    global G_N, G_ALPHA, G_RHO
    G_N = int(n)
    G_ALPHA = float(alpha)
    G_RHO = 500.0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(pen_obj, h, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=pen_grad, bounds=bounds,
                       options={'maxiter': int(iters), 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-8, 'disp': 0})
        x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return h
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return h
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def lag_grad(x, n, L):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if L == 0:
        return 1.0 - 2.0 * x
    if L > 0:
        g[L:] += 1.0 - x[:n - L]
        g[:n - L] -= x[L:]
    else:
        q = -L
        g[:n - q] += 1.0 - x[q:]
        g[q:] -= x[:n - q]
    return g


def active_idx(c, K):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    tol = max(1e-12, 0.01 * cmax)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= cmax - tol)
    if idx.size > K:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:K]]
    main = int(np.argmax(c))
    if main not in idx:
        idx = np.append(idx, main)
    return idx


def lp_step(h, n, K, trust):
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full').astype(float)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    idx = active_idx(c, K)
    if idx.size == 0:
        return h, cmax
    rows = []
    rhs = []
    for ii in idx:
        L = int(ii) - (n - 1)
        gr = lag_grad(h, n, L)
        rows.append(gr)
        rhs.append(float(np.dot(gr, h)) - float(c[ii]))
    A = np.vstack(rows).astype(float)
    b = np.asarray(rhs, dtype=float)
    A_ub = np.empty((A.shape[0], n + 1), dtype=float)
    A_ub[:, :n] = A
    A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0
    A_eq = np.ones(n + 1, dtype=float)
    A_eq[n] = 0.0
    b_eq = n / 2.0
    lb = np.maximum(0.0, h - trust)
    ub = np.minimum(1.0, h + trust)
    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        bounds.append((float(lb[i]), float(ub[i])))
    bounds.append((0.0, None))
    try:
        r = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds)
    except Exception:
        return h, cmax
    if not r.success:
        return h, cmax
    p = np.asarray(r.x[:-1], dtype=float)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = n / 2.0 - p.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        p = proj(p, n)
    best = h.copy()
    bestc = cmax
    for lam in (1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
        cand = h + lam * (p - h)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, 1.0)
        d = n / 2.0 - cand.sum()
        if abs(d) > 1e-10:
            cand = proj(cand, n)
        cc = c5(cand, n)
        if cc < bestc - 1e-13:
            bestc = cc
            best = cand.copy()
    return best, bestc


def lp_loop(h, n, deadline):
    cur = h.copy()
    curc = c5(cur, n)
    K = 28 if n < 180 else 40
    trusts = (0.10, 0.06, 0.035, 0.02, 0.012)
    for tr in trusts:
        if time.time() > deadline - 8:
            break
        for _ in range(12):
            if time.time() > deadline - 3:
                break
            nxt, nc = lp_step(cur, n, K, tr)
            if nc < curc - 1e-13:
                cur = nxt
                curc = nc
            else:
                break
    return cur, curc


def build_block(A, B, C, n, p, q, r):
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    off = 0
    if A > 0:
        h[off:off + A] = p
        off += A
    if B > 0:
        h[off:off + B] = q
        off += B
    if C > 0:
        h[off:off + C] = r
        off += C
    if B > 0:
        h[off:off + B] = q
        off += B
    if A > 0:
        h[off:off + A] = p
    return h


def block_obj(x):
    p, q = float(x[0]), float(x[1])
    A = int(GB_A)
    B = int(GB_B)
    C = int(GB_C)
    n = int(GB_N)
    if C <= 0:
        return 10.0
    r = (n / 2.0 - 2.0 * A * p - 2.0 * B * q) / C
    if p < -1e-8 or p > 1.0 + 1e-8 or q < -1e-8 or q > 1.0 + 1e-8 or r < -1e-8 or r > 1.0 + 1e-8:
        return 10.0
    p = float(np.clip(p, 0.0, 1.0))
    q = float(np.clip(q, 0.0, 1.0))
    r = float(np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0))
    h = build_block(A, B, C, n, p, q, r)
    return c5(h, n)


def low_search(n, deadline):
    global GB_N
    GB_N = int(n)
    besth = None
    bestc = 1e100
    maxA = n // 4
    maxB = n // 2
    seen = set()
    triples = []
    A0 = n // 5
    B0 = n // 5
    step = max(1, n // 20)
    for dA in range(-4, 5):
        for dB in range(-4, 5):
            A = A0 + dA * step
            B = B0 + dB * step
            C = n - 2 * A - 2 * B
            if 0 <= A <= maxA and 0 <= B <= maxB and C >= 1:
                t = (A, B, C)
                if t not in seen:
                    seen.add(t)
                    triples.append(t)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(9)
    for _ in range(170):
        A = int(rng.randint(0, maxA + 1))
        B = int(rng.randint(0, maxB + 1))
        C = n - 2 * A - 2 * B
        if C >= 1:
            t = (A, B, C)
            if t not in seen:
                seen.add(t)
                triples.append(t)
    if len(triples) > 170:
        sel = np.random.RandomState(13).choice(len(triples), 170, replace=False)
        tmp = []
        for ii in sel:
            tmp.append(triples[int(ii)])
        triples = tmp

    ps = (0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0)
    qs = (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
    for (A, B, C) in triples:
        if time.time() > deadline - 10:
            break
        GB_A, GB_B, GB_C = A, B, C
        vals = []
        starts = []
        for p in ps:
            for q in qs:
                v = block_obj((p, q))
                vals.append(v)
                starts.append((p, q))
        vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
        oi = int(np.argmin(vals))
        if vals[oi] >= 2.0:
            continue
        res = None
        try:
            res = minimize(block_obj, starts[oi], method='Nelder-Mead',
                           options={'maxfunc': 36, 'xatol': 1e-4, 'fatol': 1e-6})
        except Exception:
            res = None
        if res is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
            val = block_obj(x)
        else:
            x = np.asarray(starts[oi], dtype=float)
            val = float(vals[oi])
        if val < bestc:
            p = float(np.clip(x[0], 0.0, 1.0))
            q = float(np.clip(x[1], 0.0, 1.0))
            r = (n / 2.0 - 2.0 * A * p - 2.0 * B * q) / C
            r = float(np.clip(r, 0.0, 1.0))
            h = proj(build_block(A, B, C, n, p, q, r), n)
            cc = c5(h, n)
            if cc < bestc:
                bestc = cc
                besth = h.copy()
    return besth, bestc


def optimize_cand(hold, n, deadline):
    global G_N
    G_N = int(n)
    n = int(G_N)
    h = proj(np.asarray(hold, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = c5(h, n)
    hs = proj(sym(h), n)
    cs = c5(hs, n)
    if cs < c:
        h = hs
        c = cs

    if n > 320:
        alphas = (100.0, 500.0, 3000.0, 20000.0)
        mits = (35, 45, 60, 75)
    elif n > 170:
        alphas = (100.0, 500.0, 3000.0, 20000.0, 150000.0)
        mits = (60, 70, 90, 110, 130)
    else:
        alphas = (100.0, 500.0, 3000.0, 20000.0, 150000.0)
        mits = (80, 90, 110, 140, 140)

    for i in range(len(alphas)):
        if time.time() > deadline - 12:
            break
        x = lbfgs_step(h, n, alphas[i], mits[i])
        x = proj(x, n)
        cx = c5(x, n)
        xs = proj(sym(x), n)
        cx2 = c5(xs, n)
        if cx2 < cx:
            x = xs
            cx = cx2
        if cx < c - 1e-12:
            h = x
            c = cx

    if time.time() < deadline - 18:
        h, c = lp_loop(h, n, deadline)

    hs = proj(sym(h), n)
    cs = c5(hs, n)
    if cs < c:
        h = hs
        c = cs
    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    global BEST_H, BEST_C, BEST_N
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(40.0, float(budget_s) - 25.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    BEST_H = None
    BEST_C = 1e100
    BEST_N = 0

    h0 = None
    n0 = 0
    try:
        h0 = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        h0 = None

    if h0 is None or n0 <= 0:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)

    consider(h0, n0)

    h160 = None
    if time.time() < deadline - 220:
        dl160 = min(deadline - 180, t0 + 0.20 * max(1.0, deadline - t0))
        hl, cl = low_search(160, dl160)
        if hl is not None:
            h160 = hl
            consider(hl, 160)

    h96 = None
    if time.time() < deadline - 160:
        dl96 = min(deadline - 140, time.time() + 55.0)
        hl96, _ = low_search(96, dl96)
        if hl96 is not None:
            h96 = hl96
            consider(hl96, 96)

    tests = []
    tests.append((n0, h0.copy()))
    if h160 is not None:
        tests.append((160, h160.copy()))
    if h96 is not None:
        tests.append((96, h96.copy()))

    src = BEST_H if BEST_H is not None else h0
    srn = int(BEST_N) if BEST_N else n0
    want = (320, 256, 192, 160, 128, 96)
    for target in want:
        if len(tests) >= 6:
            break
        have = False
        for item in tests:
            if item[0] == target:
                have = True
                break
        if not have:
            tests.append((target, interp_to(src, srn, target)))

    tns = []
    ths = []
    tcs = []
    for item in tests:
        ntry = int(item[0])
        htry = proj(np.asarray(item[1], dtype=float).copy(), ntry)
        tns.append(ntry)
        ths.append(htry)
        tcs.append(c5(htry, ntry))
    tcs = np.asarray(tcs, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(tcs)

    shares = (0.34, 0.24, 0.18, 0.13, 0.10)
    rank = 0
    used = set()
    for ii in order:
        idx = int(ii)
        ntry = int(tns[idx])
        if rank >= len(shares) or ntry in used:
            continue
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        if rem < 110.0:
            break
        dl = time.time() + shares[rank] * rem
        if dl > deadline - 25.0:
            dl = deadline - 25.0
        if dl < time.time() + 45.0:
            if rank == 0:
                dl = time.time() + 45.0
            else:
                break
        ths[idx] = optimize_cand(ths[idx], ntry, dl)
        consider(ths[idx], ntry)
        used.add(ntry)
        rank += 1

    if BEST_H is not None and time.time() < deadline - 25:
        hfin = optimize_cand(BEST_H, BEST_N, deadline - 5)
        consider(hfin, BEST_N)

    if BEST_H is None:
        BEST_H = proj(h0, n0)
        BEST_C = c5(BEST_H, n0)
        BEST_N = n0

    BEST_H = proj(np.asarray(BEST_H, dtype=float).copy(), int(BEST_N))
    BEST_C = c5(BEST_H, int(BEST_N))
    return BEST_H, BEST_C, int(BEST_N)
```
